    67	## 3. Design
    68	
    69	### Part H, the harness (`scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py`)
    70	H1. **The take's evidence is parsed, not eyeballed.** `_insertion_line(text)` (proposed) parses one `insertion api=` line into `{api, route, written, returned, evidence, outcome, attempts, ms, overrun, target}`; `last_take()` returns it as `insertion` (the stale `Insertion completed via` key goes) and counts `insertion api=` lines in its window (`insertions` (proposed)). `dictate_emulator` and `debug_insert` require `insertions == 1`, `outcome == "VERIFIED"`, `written == "true"`, `target == target_package`, else `ISSUE: …` naming the field; the route is its own line `VERIFIED: route=COMMIT` (or `PASTE`, still a pass; `route` is reported, never assumed). `dictate_emulator(..., route=None)` (proposed) may REQUIRE a route: `route="COMMIT"` turns a PASTE into `ISSUE`.
    71	H2. **The content verdict is exact.** `dictate_emulator(sentence, target_package, expect=None)`: the expected editor text is `before_text + expect`, where `expect` defaults to the literal rendering rule of a plain sentence, `sentence[:1].upper() + sentence[1:] + "."` (written here as a literal, not computed by the app; if the app's cleanup changes, this goes red loudly and the caller passes `expect`). The line is `VERIFIED: the same editor's whole text now equals the expectation (N chars)` or `ISSUE: the editor's whole text differs from the expectation; it ends …`. `debug_insert(text)` compares with `before_text + text`. `_sentence_landed` is deleted (its only callers were these two); `_plain` stays for `find`.
    72	H3. **Only a uniquely focused editor is a target.** `_focused_field`: `chosen = [n for n in fields if n["focused"]]`; none → `None`; more than one → `Blocked("N editors are focused …")`. `focus_field` already reads back focus.
    73	H4. **`bound()` reads the whole block, and the exact component.** The `Bound services:{…}` entries carry the app LABEL only (`Service[label=EnviousWispr, feedbackType[…], …]`, captured 2026-09-22; no component in that block), and the component is in `Enabled services:{{com.envi.wispr/com.envi.wispr.paste.PasteAccessibilityService}}`. So: `re.search(r"Bound services:\{(.*)\}\s*$", out, re.M)` must contain `label=EnviousWispr` anywhere in the block, AND the `Enabled services` block must contain the exact `ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE` component; either missing → `False`; a missing `Bound services:` line stays `Blocked` (coverage F7, modified: the block cannot name the component, so the label is matched where it lives and the component where it lives).
    74	H5. **Evidence is bounded to the owned take.** `clear_log()` records the device clock (`date '+%m-%d %H:%M:%S.000'`) in `_STATE["log_since"]` (proposed key); `last_take()`, `recording()` and `_wait_for_the_take_to_finish` read `logcat -d -T '<since>'` when it is set and the WHOLE buffer with `"bounded": False` in the result when it is not; `last_take()` requires exactly one `recording_start` in the window, else `{"unknown": "N takes in the window"}` and the callers report `ISSUE: … UNKNOWN`.
    75	H6. **Recording off is `NOT RUN`.** Every report-returning `_recording_is_off()` caller returns `NOT RUN: {RECORDING_IS_OFF}` before staging (four suites, §10); the guard's own two raises inside `open_recorder` stay; `RECORDING_IS_OFF` text unchanged.
    76	H7. **The book is keyed by hardware identity.** Identity is cached PER TRANSPORT, never as one scalar: `_identity_of(transport)` (proposed) reads `ro.serialno` once per transport into `_STATE["identities"]` (proposed key), and `_book_key(transport)` (proposed) returns it; `_owe_locked`, `_owed`, `_settled_locked` use the key of the current transport. `restore()` (which today enumerates `_journal_read()` directly at `:3150` and compares raw keys with attached transport serials at `:3148-3158`, switching device with transport state only, `:2989-2994`) resolves EVERY attached transport to its identity under `_journal_locked`, migrates every attached transport-keyed debt under its identity key (a MOVE, never a drop), enumerates the identity-keyed debts, and uses the matching transport for the device operations that pay them; `_as_serial` carries both transport and identity. The literal `host` scope is never re-keyed. `restore()` reports which key it paid (coverage F4).
    77	H8. **An owned screen recording.** `screen_recording(path)` (proposed) context manager: journals `("screenrecord", pid_or_marker)` BEFORE `adb shell screenrecord --time-limit 180 /sdcard/wispr-eyes/<token>.mp4` starts (background), stops it with `kill -2 <pid>` found by `pidof screenrecord` (refuses when more than one), waits for the file to stop growing, pulls it to `path`, deletes the device file, settles the debt; `restore()` pays a leftover by killing and deleting. `dictate_emulator(..., record=None)`: a host path turns it on; the report carries `NOTE: screen recording at <path>` and the file's size; it is evidence to inspect, never a verdict.
    78	H9. **The speaker-playback path is deleted.** `SPEAKER_ACTIVITY`, `stage_phone_speech`, `play_staged_speech`, `unstage_phone_speech`, `say_on_phone` (`:2036`), `test_dictation` (`:3417`, which calls them at `:3474,3489,3497`) and the `dictate` help/dispatch branch (`:3591-3594`), their harness tests in `test_wispr_eyes.py`, their journal kind, and the three retired raw speaker-launch scripts `scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh`, `scripts/uat/dictate-until-silence.sh`, `scripts/uat/finish-silence-stop-uat.sh` (coverage F2); `SpeakerPlaybackActivity.java` STAYS (three instrumentation fixtures, §2.2). `RECORDING_IS_OFF` and `open_recorder` unchanged. Historical `session-log.md` entries naming them stay as history.
    79	H10. **Docs.** `wispr_eyes.py:1406` and `:3139` "dev state on a phone with no users" → "the founder's daily driver; History and foreground state are not restored; never wipe app data or uninstall `com.envi.wispr`"; `.claude/knowledge/phone-audio-playback.md` becomes a pointer to `device-testing.md` FACT: silent-physical-phone-audio-injection-for-night-uat; the skill's table gains `run_device_test` and drops `test_dictation` (`SKILL.md:22`; coverage F2). Finding 14's two other bullets are already superseded: the `tree()` claim by #181's fast eye (`device-testing.md:147-165`) and the Appium procedure by #177 (`device-testing.md:211-215`); H10 closes only the daily-driver wording and the phone-audio ownership (coverage F8).

    82	T0. **The class-wide `@Before`/`@After` go** (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:90-103` binds ASR and polish, requires the cached fixture and warms S1 for every row; coverage F1). Only `transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords` binds the services, requires the fixture and warms S1, inside itself; T1/T3/T4 require neither the fixture nor S1 when `audio=external`; `audio=speaker` alone checks the fixture with `assumeTrue`.
    83	T1. **`launcherRecordsPhonePlaybackAndReachesClipboardStep` is replaced** by `aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce` (proposed): stage `PasteTargetActivity` (field A focused, receipt file cleared by its `onCreate`); register the phase listener; start `VoiceInputActivity` (the side-button stand-in, `android-tooling.md` FACT: driving-the-app-from-the-command-line); await `LISTENING` (20 s bound, the failure names the phase seen); the audio arrives (T2); await the History row's insertion outcome through `observeAll()` (a new row with `insertionResult` in `{committed, pasted}` and `status` final; 90 s bound naming the last row seen); then assert: the receipt file's text `== EXPECTED_TEXT` (a literal, exactly once: `count(EXPECTED_TEXT) == 1`), `insertionResult == "committed"` on API 33+ with this field (the route), and `handoff` from the owner's line is not consulted for the verdict (the log stays diagnostic). No polish engine assertion (the emulator has none; the polish limb's outcome is `transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords`'s business).
    84	T2. **The audio is fed by the driver.** The test takes an instrumentation argument `audio` (`-e audio external|speaker`, default `external`): `external` means the test publishes LISTENING (it already does through the owner's phase) and WAITS; `speaker` plays the fixture through `playFixtureThroughSpeaker` (kept for the phone with no driver present; its `Thread.sleep(clip length)` is the clip's duration, not a settle, and stays). The harness gains `run_device_test(test_class, sentence, ...)` (proposed): installs nothing, runs `am instrument -w -r -e class <cls> -e audio external com.envi.wispr.test/androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner` in the background, polls the surface phase (`run-as com.envi.wispr cat shared_prefs/dictation_surface_state.xml`, a read) until `LISTENING`, then `inject_audio(pcm)` on the emulator (or, on the phone, refuses: the phone's driver is `run.py`, and `RECORDING_IS_OFF` is checked first), and returns the instrumentation's result stream parsed for the runner's status-code lines (`INSTRUMENTATION_STATUS_CODE` (external)) and the failure text. `dictate_emulator`'s 3 s warm-up before injection is kept in the driver (`grpc-take.sh`, 2026-09-13).
    85	T3. **The two-editor side-button case (REF-01).** `PasteTargetActivity` gains an intent extra `two_fields` (proposed): a second `EditText` B below A with its own receipt `paste-target-b-received.txt`. On creation the activity atomically writes `""` to BOTH receipt files BEFORE installing the text watchers (today it deletes the file and creates it only on the first change, `PasteTargetActivity.kt:55-62`; coverage F5), so an untouched B is an EXISTING empty receipt and a disabled editor write leaves A's receipt empty, which fails. Row `aTakeStartedInAAndFocusMovedToBLandsInAOnly` (proposed): start with A focused, await LISTENING, move focus to B through the activity's own runtime-registered receiver (`ACTION_FOCUS_B` (proposed), registered with `Context.RECEIVER_EXPORTED` (external) on API 33+ because the sender is the app process and the activity the test package's UID, unregistered in `onDestroy`, sent as an explicit intent with the package set to `com.envi.wispr.test`; no manifest receiver, no manifest `android:exported`; coverage F6), the audio arrives, stop; assert both receipts exist, A's `== EXPECTED_TEXT` once and B's `== ""`, `insertionResult == committed`.
    86	T4. **`aDictationWithNoFieldToInsertIntoIsNotReportedToTheUserAsAFailure`** keeps its phase waits (already subject-fired) and swaps `playFixtureThroughSpeaker()` for T2's audio argument; `observedHandoff()` (a logcat read) stays as the PRECONDITION read (it classifies the staging, it does not judge the outcome), stated in its KDoc.
    87	T5. **Class declarations.** The rewritten rows are Product Outcome (when they fail, the user's words did not reach the field they were typing in, or reached it twice, or reached the wrong field); `transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords` unchanged (Product Outcome, the polish limb).
    88	
    89	## 3b. Ownership justification
    90	No new owner. The harness stays the one driver; the device test stays the heart's one instrumented crossing; `run_device_test` is the harness driving `am instrument` the way it drives everything else.
    91	
    92	## 4. Contract deltas
    93	- `last_take()`: `insertion` becomes a dict (or `None`); new keys `insertions` (proposed), `bounded` (proposed), `unknown` (proposed).
    94	- `dictate_emulator(sentence, target_package, expect=None, route=None, record=None)`; `debug_insert(text, target_package)` verdict lines change wording (see H1/H2); the harness's own tests (`scripts/uat/test_wispr_eyes.py`) update.
    95	- `_focused_field` never returns an unfocused editor.
    96	- The restore book's keys change from transport serial to hardware identity; existing entries migrate on first read.
    97	- Recording-gated entry points return `NOT RUN: …` instead of `BLOCKED: …` (four sites).
    98	- Deleted: `SPEAKER_ACTIVITY`, `stage_phone_speech`, `unstage_phone_speech`, the wrapper at `:2038`, `_sentence_landed`, the `speaker` shell subcommands.
    99	- `VoicePipelineDeviceTest`: `launcherRecordsPhonePlaybackAndReachesClipboardStep` removed (its protection, "the launcher path reaches the clipboard step", moves into T1's stronger row: the editor's text, not a log marker; `testing-philosophy.md` RULE: deleting-a-test-carries-the-burden-of-adding-one); new rows T1, T3; instrumentation argument `audio`.
   100	- `PasteTargetActivity`: extra `two_fields`, second receipt, both receipts written `""` at creation, `ACTION_FOCUS_B` (proposed) runtime receiver (exported).
   101	- Deleted scripts: `scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh`, `scripts/uat/dictate-until-silence.sh`, `scripts/uat/finish-silence-stop-uat.sh` (raw speaker launches, superseded).
   102	- The test APK manifest: no new component (a broadcast receiver registered in the activity at runtime).
   103	
   104	## 5. End-to-end state and lifecycle audit
   105	Async edges for the device rows (`code-design-rules.md` RULE: async-edge-case-enumeration): Interrupted (the driver dies before injecting: the take ends by the 3 s silence bound or the cap; the test's 90 s wait names the phase seen; `restore()` pays the journal) · Deleted (the receipt file removed mid-run: the read fails loudly, not as `""`, because the row asserts the file EXISTS then its text) · Mutated (a second take writes the receipt: `count(EXPECTED) == 1` fails) · Concurrent (two instrumentation runs: `am instrument` serialises per runner; the harness refuses when `pidof` shows one running) · Absent (no History row within the bound: the failure names the rows seen; fail closed) · Stale (an old row: the row awaited must be CREATED after the test's start timestamp, read from `createdAtMs`).
   106	For the harness: the `log_since` (proposed) key unset → `bounded: False` in the result and the verdict says so; a journal migration racing another session → under `_journal_locked`, first-writer moves, second finds nothing to move.
   107	
   108	## 6. Downstream consumer matrix
   109	| Consumer | Reads | Change |
   110	|---|---|---|
   111	| `scripts/uat/test_wispr_eyes.py` | `_focused_field`, `last_take`, `_sentence_landed`, verdict strings | rows updated; new rows for `_insertion_line`, the exact compare, the bound window, the book key |
   112	| `scripts/uat/scenes-115/*` | `dictate_emulator`, `last_take()['ended_by']` | unchanged keys; re-run one scene |
   113	| `.claude/skills/wispr-eyes/SKILL.md`, `device-testing.md` | the call table, `stage_phone_speech` | speaker rows removed, `run_device_test` added |
   114	| `scripts/uat/silent-audio/run.py` | nothing from the harness | unchanged |
   115	| `SilenceStoppedTakeTranscribesDeviceTest` | `SpeakerPlaybackActivity` | unchanged (the activity stays) |
   116	
   117	## 7. Failure-mode × caller table
   118	| Failure | `dictate_emulator` | `run_device_test` | `restore()` |
   119	|---|---|---|---|
   120	| no `insertion api=` line in the window | `ISSUE: no insertion outcome was logged for this take` | the row's own 90 s bound fails naming the rows seen | n/a |
   121	| two takes in the window | `ISSUE: … UNKNOWN (2 takes)` | same | n/a |
   122	| editor text ≠ expectation | `ISSUE: … differs; it ends …` | the row fails with both strings | n/a |
   123	| no focused editor / two focused | `BLOCKED` before the take | the rig focuses A itself | n/a |
   124	| `screenrecord` left running | n/a | n/a | killed by pid, file deleted, debt settled, reported |
   125	| `ro.serialno` unreadable | `device()` refuses (identity unknown) | same | pays the transport-keyed book and says so |
   126	
   127	## 8. Caller-visible signals audit
   128	Verdict lines are the signals: `VERIFIED:` only on the exact editor text AND the parsed outcome; `ISSUE:` names the field that failed; `NOT RUN:` for recording off; `BLOCKED:` for a precondition the caller can fix; `NOTE:` for evidence (the route line, the recording path).
   129	
   130	## 9. Fallback source-of-truth audit
   131	The editor's own text (receipt file on the rig; the tree read on Gmail) is the truth for "landed"; the History row is the truth for the route; the log is diagnostic only. Where they disagree the row fails and prints all three.
   132	
   133	## 10. File-by-file changes
   134	- `scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py`: H1..H10. `_recording_is_off()` callers, enumerated (`grep -n '_recording_is_off()' scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py`, 2026-09-22): six sites; the four report-returning suites at `:2595` (`dictate_emulator`), `:3176` (`check_recorder`), `:3388` (`room_is_quiet`), `:3438` (`test_dictation`) change to `NOT RUN:`; the two `raise Blocked(RECORDING_IS_OFF)` inside `open_recorder` (`:1674`, `:1741`) STAY: they are the guard itself, and a context manager cannot return a report.
   135	- `scripts/uat/test_wispr_eyes.py`: rows per §6.
   136	- `app/src/androidTest/java/com/envi/wispr/VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt`: T0, T1, T2, T4, T5.
   137	- `scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh`, `scripts/uat/dictate-until-silence.sh`, `scripts/uat/finish-silence-stop-uat.sh`: deleted (H9).
   138	- `app/src/androidTest/java/com/envi/wispr/PasteTargetActivity.kt`: T3.
   139	- `.claude/skills/wispr-eyes/SKILL.md`, `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md`, `.claude/knowledge/phone-audio-playback.md`: H10 and the `run_device_test` recipe.
   140	
   141	## 11. Testing
   142	1. Classes: the harness's JVM rows (`test_wispr_eyes.py`) are Harness Contract; T1 and T3 are Product Outcome (a user's words reach the field they were typing in, once, and no other); T4 stays Product Outcome; the deleted launcher row's protection moves into T1.
   143	2. Two-way controls for the harness parser: `_insertion_line` against the live line captured in §2.5 (parses every field) and against a line with `outcome=UNVERIFIED` (the verdict is `ISSUE`).
   144	
   145	### 11.1 Hardware UAT spec (emulator)
   146	- `dictate_emulator('the quarterly marker sentence lands tomorrow', route='COMMIT')` into Gmail: `VERIFIED: route=COMMIT`, `VERIFIED: … whole text now equals the expectation`, and with `expect='wrong'` the same take reports `ISSUE` (the negative control, run once).
   147	- `run_device_test('com.envi.wispr.VoicePipelineDeviceTest#aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce', …)` and `…#aTakeStartedInAAndFocusMovedToBLandsInAOnly`: both green on the emulator; the failure control: run T1 with the harness injecting NOTHING (the take ends on the bound) and read the row red naming the phase seen.
   148	- `screen_recording` around one take: the file pulled, non-empty, playable (`ffprobe` duration ≈ take length), the device file gone, the book empty.
   149	- `restore()` after a simulated leak: start `screenrecord` through the helper, kill the Python process, run `restore()` from a fresh process: killed, deleted, reported.
   150	
   151	### 11.2 Other obligations
   152	| Test | Class | Proves | Revert that turns it red |
   153	|---|---|---|---|
   154	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_insertion_line_parses_the_live_shape` (proposed) | Harness Contract | every field of the captured line | drop a field |
   155	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_verdict_requires_verified_written_and_target` (proposed) | Harness Contract | `outcome=UNVERIFIED`, `written=false`, a foreign target each give `ISSUE` | gate on `outcome` only |
   156	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_whole_text_must_equal_before_plus_expected` (proposed) | Harness Contract | a superset, a duplicate, a truncation each give `ISSUE` | fall back to substring |
   157	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_only_a_focused_editor_is_chosen` (proposed) | Harness Contract | one unfocused editor → `None`; two focused → `Blocked` | `focused or fields` |
   158	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_bound_reads_the_whole_block` (proposed) | Harness Contract | ours second in the block → `True`; absent → `False`; no block → `Blocked` | anchor at the head |
   159	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_book_is_keyed_by_identity_and_migrates` (proposed) | Harness Contract | a transport-keyed debt is paid under the identity key; nothing dropped | key by serial |
   160	| `test_wispr_eyes.py::test_recording_off_is_not_run` (proposed) | Harness Contract | all four gated entry points return `NOT RUN:` | `BLOCKED:` at one |
   161	| `VoicePipelineDeviceTest.aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce` (proposed) | Product Outcome | receipt `== EXPECTED_TEXT` once, `insertionResult == committed` | write the text twice; disable the editor write (the row fails even though logs remain) |
   162	| `VoicePipelineDeviceTest.aTakeStartedInAAndFocusMovedToBLandsInAOnly` (proposed) | Product Outcome | A holds the text, B is empty | insert into the focused field |
   163	
   164	## 12. Blast radius & rollback
   165	Dev tooling and instrumentation only; the app APK does not change (the test APK does). Rollback: revert the PR. Risk: the restore-book re-key touches the founder's phone's journal; the migration runs under the existing lock and is covered by a JVM row against a copied book; `restore()` prints the key it paid.
   166	
   167	## 13. Ship criteria specific to THIS change
   168	- `python3 scripts/uat/test_wispr_eyes.py` green with the new rows, count reported.
   169	- The emulator UAT of §11.1, including the two negative controls.
   170	- Codex ALL-CLEAR with a confirming rerun; Phase 3 PASS; PR "Closes #161".
   171	
   172	## 14. Open questions
   173	- The founder's phone pass: T1 and T3 with `-e audio external` need the phone's driver; `run_device_test` refuses on the phone while `RECORDING_IS_OFF`, so on the phone the rows run with `-e audio speaker` by hand, or through `run.py` once it learns to drive `am instrument`. Deferred to his pass.
   174	- REF-02's injected settings/vocabulary failure rows on the device: deferred. No seam exists (`grep -rln 'FailOpen\|failOpen\|DebugFailure' app/src/main/java app/src/debug/java` → none), a seam on a guard is a bypass (`validation-discipline.md` RULE: a-test-seam-on-a-GUARD-is-a-bypass), and corrupting the founder's preference files is not a staging step; #193's JVM rows are the evidence.
   175	
   176	## 15. Related
   177	#161 (this), #162 (`run.py`), #177 (one door), #181 (fast eye), #192 (REF-01, the pin), #193 (REF-02), #204, #206; audit `docs/audits/2026-09-20-senior-audit.md` REF-09.

  1432	def _dictation(what):
  1433	    """Send start / stop / cancel through the ONE door adb can reach.
  1434	
  1435	    `DictationSessionService` is NOT exported, so `am start-service` at it is refused
  1436	    ("Permission Denial: ... not exported"). `VoiceInputActivity` IS exported and is the Samsung
  1437	    side-button trampoline: it reads `stop`, `cancel` and `toggle` boolean extras and forwards the
  1438	    matching command to the service. So this is not a back door, it is the same door the side button
  1439	    uses. Measured 2026-09-06 against `VoiceInputActivity.kt` and the manifest.
  1440	    """
  1441	    # STARTING A TAKE IS NOT AVAILABLE HERE, and that is a deletion rather than a guard.
  1442	    #
  1443	    # Two review rounds each found a different path that reached this function with "start", got past
  1444	    # whatever cleanup that caller had, and returned with the microphone open. The third one was found
  1445	    # after the fix for the second, which is the signal that the SHAPE is wrong: any number of callers
  1446	    # can start a take, and each one has to remember to end it. So the shape is gone. `open_recorder` is
  1447	    # a context manager that owns a take from its first instruction to its last, and it is the only
  1448	    # thing that can begin one.
  1449	    if what not in ("stop", "cancel"):
  1450	        raise Blocked("a take is started only by `open_recorder()`, which owns ending it")
  1451	    flag = {"stop": "--ez stop true", "cancel": "--ez cancel true"}[what]
  1452	    remote, out = _adb(f"am start -n {shlex.quote(RECORDER_ACTIVITY)} {flag}".strip(), check=False)
  1453	    if remote != 0 or "Error" in out:
  1454	        detail = out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else "no message"
  1455	        raise Blocked(f"the recorder would not accept {what!r}: {detail}")
  1456	    time.sleep(1.5)
  1457	    _STATE["tree"] = None

  1460	def recording():
  1461	    """Is a dictation live RIGHT NOW, read from the two lines the capture itself writes.
  1462	
  1463	    `AudioCapture` writes `recording_start` when the microphone opens and `recording_stop` when it
  1464	    closes, so a start with no later stop IS a live take. The subject is the witness, which is the
  1465	    property that matters here: no other signal is written by the thing actually holding the microphone.
  1466	
  1467	    **The notification was tried first and is a trap.** `dumpsys notification` keeps our entry in its
  1468	    dump after the take has ended, so counting mentions of the package answered "yes, recording" a full
  1469	    two seconds after the log had already recorded `recording_stop`. A plausible number about the wrong
  1470	    thing (`validation-discipline.md`, plausible-value traps).
  1471	
  1472	    Answers False when the log holds neither line, which is the honest reading of "nothing has started
  1473	    a take since the log was last cleared".
  1474	    """
  1475	    text = logs(lines=600)
  1476	    last_start = last_stop = -1
  1477	    for index, line in enumerate(text.splitlines()):
  1478	        if "recording_start" in line:
  1479	            last_start = index
  1480	        elif "recording_stop" in line:
  1481	            last_stop = index
  1482	    if last_start < 0 and last_stop < 0:
  1483	        # NEITHER LINE IS THERE, which is not "no take is running". It is what a cleared log looks like,
  1484	        # and what a take whose start scrolled out of the window looks like, and answering False sends a
  1485	        # caller away believing the microphone is closed.
  1486	        raise Blocked(
  1487	            "the log holds neither the start nor the end of a take, so whether the microphone is open "
  1488	            "cannot be read. Clear the log before the take you mean to measure."
  1489	        )
  1490	    return last_start > last_stop

  1503	def toggle_dictation():
  1504	    """The side button pressed AGAIN during a take: the bare toggle, the same intent the Samsung side
  1505	    button sends, with no `stop` extra. It is the only way to drive the launcher's stop path (#192: the

  1517	def _newest_recording_start():
  1518	    """The newest `recording_start` line the capture wrote, or None when the tail holds none.
  1519	
  1520	    The IDENTITY of that line is the witness a rolling tail cannot fake: a new take writes a new line
  1521	    with its own timestamp, while a tail that rolled older lines out still ends at the same newest line.
  1522	    A count of lines across two snapshots can read equal or lower after a new start (round G2).
  1523	    """
  1524	    lines = [line for line in logs(lines=600).splitlines() if "recording_start" in line]

  1528	def _press_launcher(what, flag, deadline_s=3.0):
  1529	    """Send one launcher press during a live take and prove afterwards that it did not begin a take.
  1530	
  1531	    The liveness check and the intent are two steps. A take ending between the liveness check and intent
  1532	    delivery turns the toggle into a start. The proof is the newest
  1533	    `recording_start` line before the press against the newest one seen during `deadline_s` after it
  1534	    (`_newest_recording_start`): a different line is a take this press began, which is cancelled before
  1535	    raising; a tail with no start line at all after the press is "cannot tell", which also raises, never
  1536	    "no new take". One quiet observation is not proof while a take can still be STARTING, so the whole
  1537	    deadline is watched.

  1556	        if newest is None:

  1571	    was busy being careful about a setting. So the order is: end the recording, THEN try the repair, and
  1572	    the repair's failure is raised after the microphone is already closed.

  1575	    # the one most likely to be followed by the process ending. Journaled, so a later session can put it
  1576	    # back even if this one does not survive to.

  1588	        unreadable = why

  1590	        # clears the accessibility settings and their previous values could not be read, so there is

  1664	    found after the first was fixed. Any number of callers being able to begin a take, each responsible
  1665	    for remembering to end it, is the defect; so beginning one is no longer something a caller can do.

  1720	    # what makes the window itself not exist, because no other process can read or settle the book
  1721	    # until the take is real. The cost is that a second session waits for this one, which on a harness
  1722	    # with exactly one phone is a description of the correct behaviour rather than a price.
  1723	    # THE LOCK IS TAKEN HERE, INSIDE, AND NOT BY A DECORATOR. `@_atomic_change` on a
  1724	    # `@contextmanager` wraps the call that BUILDS the generator, so it acquires and releases the lock

  1726	    # commit message of mine said the book was held for the whole take. It was not. A decorator cannot
  1727	    # hold a lock across a `yield`; only a `with` inside the generator can.

  1732	                          "nothing has written down is one nobody can end.")
  1733	        # THE START IS WRITTEN HERE AND NOWHERE ELSE. It used to be a function of its own, and a
  1734	        # function that starts a recording is one somebody can call: with the off-switch removed it
  1735	        # would send the start with no debt and no cleanup, which is the whole class again wearing the
  1736	        # shape of a helper. There is no such function now.
  1737	        #

  1739	        # reached the phone, so settling the debt on the way out is exactly the case the debt exists
  1740	        # for: the take runs and the book forgets it. An uncertain start keeps its debt.
  1741	        if _recording_is_off():
  1742	            raise Blocked(RECORDING_IS_OFF)
  1743	        remote, out = _adb(f"am start -n {shlex.quote(RECORDER_ACTIVITY)}", check=False)
  1744	        if remote != 0 or "Error" in out:
  1745	            detail = out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else "no message"
  1746	            raise Blocked(f"the recorder would not start: {detail}")
  1747	        time.sleep(1.5)
  1748	        _STATE["tree"] = None
  1749	        try:
  1750	            time.sleep(1.0)
  1751	            _STATE["tree"] = None
  1752	            pill = None
  1753	            if verify:
  1754	                pill = overlay()
  1755	                if pill is None:
  1756	                    raise Blocked(
  1757	                        "the recorder was started but no pill is in the accessibility tree, and the "
  1758	                        "window manager has no recorder window either, so the take did not draw. It has "
  1759	                        "been cancelled, so nothing is still listening."
  1760	                    )

  1767	            # return while a take might still be running, so reaching here means it is not.

  2571	def focus_field(label, package):
  2572	    """Press an editor by its hint or text and READ BACK that it took focus.
  2573	
  2574	    An editor is pressable by definition, whatever the tree says about `clickable`, so the press lands
  2575	    on the label's own centre. The read-back is the point: a dictation aims at the focused field, and a
  2576	    press that did not focus it would send the words somewhere else.
  2577	    """
  2578	    tap(label, clickable=False, package=package)
  2579	    time.sleep(0.8)
  2580	    field = _focused_field(package)
  2581	    if field is None:
  2582	        raise Blocked(f"{label!r} was pressed but no editor in {package} has focus")
  2583	    return f"focused {label!r} ({field[1]})"

  2586	def dictate_emulator(sentence="and I will send the deck tomorrow", target_package="com.google.android.gm"):
  2587	    """One spoken take on the EMULATOR, fed over gRPC, judged by the editor's own text.
  2588	
  2589	    Replaces `scripts/uat/grpc-take.sh`, which tapped coordinates it had worked out by hand, turned the
  2590	    host microphone off and never back on, and trusted a log line. Here the take is owned by
  2591	    `open_recorder()`, the microphone by `set_host_mic()` and its journal, and the verdict by the field
  2592	    that was focused before the take and is still the same field after it.
  2593	    """
  2594	    _require_emulator("a spoken take")
  2595	    if _recording_is_off():
  2596	        return [f"BLOCKED: {RECORDING_IS_OFF}"]
  2597	    report = []
  2598	    restore()
  2599	    ready()
  2600	    if not bound():
  2601	        return ["BLOCKED: auto-paste is switched off, so nothing can be inserted. "
  2602	                "enable_auto_paste() turns it back on."]
  2603	    before = _focused_field(target_package)
  2604	    if before is None:
  2605	        return [f"BLOCKED: no focused editor in {target_package} is on screen; tap into one first"]
  2606	    scratch = os.path.join(os.environ.get("TMPDIR", "/tmp"), "wispr-eyes")
  2607	    os.makedirs(scratch, exist_ok=True)
  2608	    pcm = _pcm_from_sentence(sentence, os.path.join(scratch, f"utt-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.pcm"))
  2609	    report.append(f"NOTE: {sentence!r} was rendered to {pcm}")
  2610	    ending = None
  2611	    try:
  2612	        # OFF AS THE RESTING STATE, never a switch inside the take: see _rest_host_mic_off. Done before
  2613	        # the audio is rendered so the switch, if one happens at all, is as far from the capture as the
  2614	        # call allows.
  2615	        report.append(f"NOTE: {_rest_host_mic_off()}")
  2616	        clear_log()
  2617	        with open_recorder(verify=False):
  2618	            time.sleep(3.0)  # the recogniser warms; injecting sooner is dropped (grpc-take.sh, 2026-09-13)
  2619	            report.append(f"NOTE: {inject_audio(pcm)}")
  2620	            time.sleep(2.0)
  2621	            stop_dictation()
  2622	            ending = _wait_for_the_take_to_finish()
  2623	    finally:
  2624	        for line in restore():
  2625	            report.append(f"NOTE: restored {line}")
  2626	    if ending is None:
  2627	        report.append("ISSUE: the dictation never reached an ending, so nothing can be said about it")
  2628	        return report
  2629	    take = last_take()
  2630	    report.append(f"VERIFIED: a take ran, ended by {take['ended_by']}" if take["ended_by"]
  2631	                  else "ISSUE: no take ending was logged")
  2632	    if take["transcribed_chars"]:
  2633	        report.append(f"VERIFIED: {take['transcribed_chars']} characters came back from the speech engine")
  2634	    else:
  2635	        report.append("ISSUE: nothing was transcribed; either the stream never reached the microphone "
  2636	                      "or the engine returned empty")
  2637	    _, route = _adb("logcat -d | grep 'insertion api=' | grep -v adbd | tail -1", check=False)
  2638	    if route.strip():
  2639	        report.append(f"NOTE: {route.strip()[-160:]}")
  2640	    after = _focused_field(target_package)
  2641	    if after is None:
  2642	        report.append("ISSUE: no focused editor is on screen after the take, so where the words went "
  2643	                      "is unknown")
  2644	    elif after[:2] != before[:2]:
  2645	        report.append("ISSUE: a DIFFERENT editor is focused now, so nothing here says where the words went")
  2646	    elif after[2] == before[2]:
  2647	        report.append(f"ISSUE: the editor is unchanged; it still holds {after[2]!r}")
  2648	    else:
  2649	        if not _sentence_landed(sentence, after[2]):
  2650	            report.append(f"ISSUE: the editor changed but does not hold the sentence in order; it ends "
  2651	                          f"{_excerpt(after[2])}")
  2652	        else:
  2653	            report.append(f"VERIFIED: the same editor now holds the sentence; it ends {_excerpt(after[2])}")
  2654	    return report

  2657	def debug_insert(text, target_package=None):
  2658	    """Insert a sentence through the app's real insertion path with NO audio, on the EMULATOR's DEBUG build.
  2659	
  2660	    Replaces `scripts/uat/debug-insert.sh`. The debug-only broadcast pins the focused editor and
  2661	    commits or pastes exactly as a take would, so it drives the caret matrix deterministically. The
  2662	    editor's own text is the verdict, never the clipboard.
  2663	    """
  2664	    _require_emulator("the debug insert")
  2665	    ready()
  2666	    if not bound():
  2667	        return ["BLOCKED: auto-paste is switched off, so nothing can be inserted"]
  2668	    # THE PACKAGE's flag line is the bracketed one; the first bare `flags=0x0` belongs to a component
  2669	    # and read as "not debuggable" on the first live run (2026-09-20).
  2670	    _, dump = _adb(f"dumpsys package {PACKAGE} | grep -m1 DEBUGGABLE", check=False)
  2671	    if "DEBUGGABLE" not in dump:
  2672	        return [f"BLOCKED: the installed {PACKAGE} is not a debug build, so the insert receiver is absent"]
  2673	    before = _focused_field(target_package)
  2674	    if before is None:
  2675	        return ["BLOCKED: no focused editor is on screen; tap into one first"]
  2676	    clear_log()
  2677	    _adb(f"am broadcast -a {PACKAGE}.debug.INSERT --es text {shlex.quote(text)} {PACKAGE}")
  2678	    time.sleep(4)
  2679	    report = []
  2680	    # `grep -v adbd`: adbd logs the shell command it was asked to run, pattern included, so without it
  2681	    # the last line read back is this very grep.
  2682	    _, lines = _adb("logcat -d | grep -E 'insertion api=|DebugInsert: pin=' | grep -v adbd | tail -2",
  2683	                    check=False)
  2684	    for line in lines.strip().splitlines():
  2685	        report.append(f"NOTE: {line.strip()[-160:]}")
  2686	    after = _focused_field(target_package)
  2687	    if after is None or after[:2] != before[:2]:
  2688	        report.append("ISSUE: the focused editor changed or vanished, so where the text went is unknown")
  2689	    elif text in (after[2] or ""):
  2690	        report.append(f"VERIFIED: the same editor now holds the text; it ends {_excerpt(after[2])}")
  2691	    else:
  2692	        report.append(f"ISSUE: the editor reads {after[2]!r}, which does not contain {text!r}")
  2693	    return report

  2858	def clear_log():
  2859	    """Clear the ring buffer so an absent line means absent rather than left over.
  2860	
  2861	    **IT CHANGES THE PHONE AND CANNOT BE PUT BACK, and that is stated rather than journaled.** The log
  2862	    it discards is gone; a debt promising to restore it would be a lie, and a lie in the restore book is
  2863	    worse than an honest gap. What is lost is diagnostic history, never the founder's own data.
  2864	
  2865	    **It no longer RESIZES the buffer, and that is a deletion rather than a fix.** The old version
  2866	    widened it to 16M and journaled "the buffer was N K" from the first line of `logcat -g` — which
  2867	    names one buffer out of several, and silently journaled nothing at all when that line did not match,
  2868	    while still doing the resize. A debt that cannot describe the change it undoes is worse than no
  2869	    resize: it leaves the founder's phone altered and the book saying otherwise. Every take this tool
  2870	    measures is seconds long, so the default buffer holds it.
  2871	    """
  2872	    _adb("logcat -c")
  2873

  2875	def keep_awake(minutes=30):
  2876	    """Hold the screen on for a long walk, and journal the old timeout so `restore()` puts it back.
  2877	
  2878	    A walk of every page takes longer than the founder's screen timeout, and a phone that sleeps
  2879	    mid-walk produces a refusal about the lock screen rather than an answer about the page. This is a
  2880	    change to HIS daily driver, so it is journaled like any other
  2881	    (`session-behavior.md` RULE: revert-the-phone-after-a-session).
  2882	
  2883	    It does NOT touch the lock itself. A locked phone is refused, never opened: waking and swiping does
  2884	    not defeat a secure lock, and a harness that tries is doing something nobody asked it to.
  2885	    """
  2886	    if not isinstance(minutes, int) or isinstance(minutes, bool) or not 1 <= minutes <= 60:
  2887	        raise Blocked("minutes must be a whole number from 1 to 60")
  2888	    # UNDER THE LOCK, for the same reason `restore()` is: another session could settle this debt in the
  2889	    # gap between writing it and making the change, leaving the founder's screen timeout altered with
  2890	    # nothing recording it.
  2891	    with _journal_locked():
  2892	        _, previous = _adb("settings get system screen_off_timeout")
  2893	        previous = previous.strip()
  2894	        if not previous.isdigit():
  2895	            raise Blocked(
  2896	                f"could not read the phone's screen timeout, so it could not be safely changed: {previous!r}")
  2897	        # Owed BEFORE the write. A note about a change that did not happen is a wasted restore; a change
  2898	        # with no note is a setting left on the founder's phone.
  2899	        _owe_locked(("screen-timeout", previous), device())
  2900	        wanted = str(minutes * 60_000)
  2901	        _adb(f"settings put system screen_off_timeout {wanted}")
  2902	        _, actual = _adb("settings get system screen_off_timeout")
  2903	        if actual.strip() != wanted:
  2904	            raise Blocked("the screen timeout did not change, so the note about it would be wrong")
  2905	        return f"screen stays on for {minutes} minutes (was {int(previous) // 1000}s)"
  2906

  2908	def logs(pattern=None, lines=200):
  2909	    """The app's own log lines, newest last.
  2910	
  2911	    `lines` is validated rather than interpolated: it reaches a shell command, and a string there would
  2912	    carry whatever the caller put in it.
  2913	    """
  2914	    if not isinstance(lines, int) or isinstance(lines, bool) or not 1 <= lines <= 10000:
  2915	        raise Blocked("lines must be a whole number from 1 to 10000")
  2916	    # LOGCAT'S OWN TAG FILTER, not a pipe into grep. Two things were wrong with the pipe: a pipeline
  2917	    # reports its LAST command's status, so a failed `logcat` looked like a successful empty read, and
  2918	    # `check=False` then turned that into "nothing was logged" — which `recording()` reads as "the
  2919	    # microphone is closed". The filter also cannot be defeated by a tag containing a regex character.
  2920	    filters = " ".join(shlex.quote(f"{tag}:V") for tag in APP_TAGS)
  2921	    _, out = _adb(f"logcat -d -v time {filters} {shlex.quote('*:S')}", timeout=90)
  2922	    selected = out.splitlines()[-lines:]
  2923	    if pattern:
  2924	        selected = [line for line in selected if re.search(pattern, line)]
  2925	    return "\n".join(selected).strip()
  2926	
  2927	
  2928	def last_take():
  2929	    """What the most recent dictation did, as a small dictionary a person can read.
  2930	
  2931	    Every field comes from a line the app itself wrote. A field is None when the app never said it,
  2932	    which is different from zero and is left different.
  2933	    """
  2934	    text = logs(lines=400)
  2935	    # ONE TAKE, THE LAST ONE. Every field below is the FIRST match in the text, so with two takes in
  2936	    # the log a run could report one take's ending beside another take's character count and read as a
  2937	    # single coherent result.
  2938	    starts = list(re.finditer(r"(?m)^.*\brecording_start\b.*$", text))
  2939	    if not starts:
  2940	        raise Blocked("the log holds no take at all since it was last cleared, so there is nothing to "
  2941	                      "report on. Call clear_log() before the take you mean to measure.")
  2942	    text = text[starts[-1].start():]
  2943	
  2944	    def first(pattern, cast=str):
  2945	        match = re.search(pattern, text)
  2946	        if not match:
  2947	            return None
  2948	        try:
  2949	            return cast(match.group(1))
  2950	        except (ValueError, IndexError):
  2951	            return None
  2952	    return {
  2953	        "started": "recording_start" in text,
  2954	        "ended_by": first(r"Stopped by ([^.]+)\."),
  2955	        "bytes": first(r"Stopped by [^.]+\. (\d+) bytes", int),
  2956	        "seconds": first(r"\((\d+\.\d+)s\)", float),
  2957	        "warned_at_ms": first(r"Duration warning shown at (\d+)ms", int),
  2958	        "hit_the_cap": "Take ended at the duration cap" in text,
  2959	        "transcribed_chars": first(r"Transcription result received \(chars=(\d+)\)", int),
  2960	        "handoff": first(r"handoff=(\w+)"),
  2961	        "insertion": first(r"Insertion completed via (\w+)"),
  2962	    }
  2963

    75	and asserts the wrong thing is repaired in place. A fixture whose subject DOES NOT EXIST in that
    76	configuration cannot be repaired at all — editing the assertion turns it into a passing test about nothing.
    77	
    78	**A failing new test must name WHAT IT FOUND**, not merely that an expectation failed.
    79	
    80	## RULE: do-not-multiply-independent-axes
    81	Test every BRANCH on one ordinary input, then each special input CLASS at one representative branch. Never
    82	the Cartesian product. A row earns its place only by reaching a different production branch or reproducing
    83	a distinct OBSERVED defect.
    84	
    85	## RULE: never-guess-when-the-subject-is-finished
    86	A test learns the subject is done from a signal the SUBJECT fires — never elapsed time, a `delay()`, a
    87	poll, or the harness going quiet.
    88	
    89	**A COUNT THAT EXHAUSTS IS INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM THE CONDITION IT WAS MEASURING.** A loop inferring "the
    90	stop never arrived" from a budget running out accuses production code when the harness merely ran out of
    91	budget. Do not close one by raising the cap.
    92	
    93	**The same missing signal produces the VACUITY direction, which is worse because nothing goes red.** Flake
    94	and vacuity are one defect wearing two symptoms.
    95	
    96	## RULE: a-flaky-suite-earns-no-new-cases-until-its-wait-seam-is-fixed
    97	The next change to a flaking suite is the seam fix. Not a rerun, not a longer timeout, not another case.
    98	**Raising a wait budget is a silent coverage loss unless you name a regression the old value would have
    99	caught and the new one would not.** This bites hardest on device tests, where a real timing bug and a slow
   100	phone look identical.
   101	
   102	## RULE: deleting-a-test-carries-the-burden-of-adding-one
   103	Name what it protected, then either why that protection is no longer needed or where it now lives. "It
   104	never caught anything" is not evidence — a good test never catching anything is the expected case. Never
   105	delete or weaken a guard to move a number.
    20	
    21	## RULE: one-device-means-check-before-you-claim-it
    22	The phone serialises every session. Before installing or driving it, probe for another session's run.
    23	Where ownership is unclear or several candidates match, stop rather than pick one.
    24	
    25	An empty process table is not evidence of a release: taking the slot BEGINS by creating that absence.
    26	
    27	## RULE: identify-a-process-by-path-never-by-name
    28	Never kill by bare process name. Identify every candidate by process id and full command. A start time is
    29	falsifiable against your own history; a name is not.
    30	
    31	## RULE: a-harness-that-acts-must-refuse-not-choose
    32	A harness that kills, installs, or wipes is worse in kind than one that reports wrongly, because it acts
    33	on the guess, unattended.
    34	
    35	- When more than one target matches, return invalid with the count and the candidates.
    36	- Every kill names a process id verified in the same call.
    37	- A harness that mutates the device or the launch environment must restore it.
    38	
    39	## RULE: never-unattended-recursive-delete
    40	Never write an unattended recursive delete over a path a user could have touched, on the host or on the
    41	device. The ways a mount or a link can be in the way are not enumerable by testing the path. Enumerate
    42	entries, delete individually, and assert the world afterwards rather than trusting an exit code.
    43	
    44	## RULE: a-backgrounded-subshell-outlives-the-tool-call-that-made-it
    45	Never generate machine load with `( … ) &` inside a Bash call: the shell exits, every child is reparented

  3216	_TERMINAL_LINES = (
  3217	    "Auto-insert handed", "kept on clipboard", "Transcription failed",
  3218	    "Speech recognition failed", "No audio captured", "showError",
  3219	    # `Take terminal: <STATE>` is the session's own ending line (seen `NO_SPEECH` on 2026-09-20); without
  3220	    # it a take that heard nothing waited the full 40 s and 200 log reads before saying so.

  3224	
  3225	def _wait_for_the_take_to_finish(seconds=40):
  3226	    """Poll the log until the dictation reaches an ending, and say which one. None on a timeout."""
  3227	    for _ in range(seconds * 2):
  3228	        time.sleep(0.5)
  3229	        text = logs(lines=200)
  3230	        for marker in _TERMINAL_LINES:

     1	package com.envi.wispr.ui
     2	
     3	import android.Manifest
     4	import android.app.Activity
     5	import android.content.Intent
     6	import android.content.pm.PackageManager

     8	import android.view.Gravity
     9	import android.view.WindowManager
    10	import android.widget.Toast
    11	import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
    12	
    13	/** Samsung side-button trampoline. It never owns a recording session or visible window. */
    14	class VoiceInputActivity : Activity() {
    15	    companion object {
    16	        const val EXTRA_STOP = "stop"
    17	        const val EXTRA_CANCEL = "cancel"
    18	        const val EXTRA_TOGGLE = "toggle"
    19	
    20	        /**
    21	         * Start, never toggle. The floating bubble sends this: a TOGGLE arriving while a take is still
    22	         * STARTING would cancel it, and a second tap during startup is exactly what a user does when
    23	         * nothing has visibly happened yet.
    24	         */
    25	        const val EXTRA_START = "start"
    26	
    27	        /** The bubble's request token, forwarded onto the service intent unchanged. */
    28	        const val EXTRA_REQUEST = "request"
    29	
    30	        /**
    31	         * `TriggerSource.wire` from a surface that launches this window (the tile, the app). Absent on
    32	         * the side button, which arrives as `ACTION_ASSIST` and is read as such; absent on anything else
    33	         * (an `am start` from a harness) is `unknown`, never a guess (issue #176).
    34	         */
    35	        const val EXTRA_TRIGGER_SOURCE = "trigger_source"
    36	
    37	        /** The surface this launch names, from its extra or its action. Pure, for `TriggerSourceTest`. */
    38	        internal fun triggerOf(action: String?, extra: String?): TriggerSource = when {
    39	            extra != null -> TriggerSource.fromExtra(extra)
    40	            action == Intent.ACTION_ASSIST -> TriggerSource.ASSIST
    41	            else -> TriggerSource.UNKNOWN
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    46	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    47	        configureLauncherWindow()
    48	        if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) !=
    49	            PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
    50	        ) {
    51	            Toast.makeText(this, "Allow microphone access in EnviousWispr first", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
    52	            startActivity(
    53	                Intent(this, SettingsActivity::class.java)
    54	                    .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP),
    55	            )
    56	            finishWithoutAnimation()
    57	            return
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        val action = when {
    61	            intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_CANCEL, false) -> DictationSessionService.ACTION_CANCEL
    62	            intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_STOP, false) -> DictationSessionService.ACTION_STOP
    63	            intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_START, false) -> DictationSessionService.ACTION_START
    64	            intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_TOGGLE, false) -> DictationSessionService.ACTION_TOGGLE
    65	            else -> DictationSessionService.ACTION_TOGGLE
    66	        }
    67	        // No pin here. The session owner makes the single pin attempt in `beginSession`, and that
    68	        // result is the record every announcement is judged against. A pin taken by this window ran
    69	        // on every START or TOGGLE press, including the TOGGLE that stops a take, and moved the words
    70	        // to whichever field the user had reached by then (#192).
    71	        val trigger = triggerOf(intent.action, intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TRIGGER_SOURCE))
    72	        runCatching { DictationSessionService.sendCommand(this, action, intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_REQUEST), trigger) }
    73	            .onFailure {
    74	                Toast.makeText(this, "Dictation service could not start", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
    75	            }
    76	        finishWithoutAnimation()
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    private fun configureLauncherWindow() {
    80	        window.clearFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_DIM_BEHIND)
    81	        window.addFlags(
    82	            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or
    83	                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or
    84	                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL,
    85	        )
    86	        window.attributes = window.attributes.apply {
    87	            width = 1
    88	            height = 1
    89	            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
    90	            x = 0
    91	            y = 0
    92	        }
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private fun finishWithoutAnimation() {
    96	        finish()
    97	        overridePendingTransition(0, 0)
    98	    }
    99	}

   640	        // padding is exactly the bubble's footprint, under the finger that is holding it.

   442	    private fun digitScales(input: String): String = Regex(
   443	        "(?i)\\b(\\d[\\d,]*\\s+(?:hundred|thousand)(?:\\s+(?:$numberAlt))*)\\b",
   444	    ).replace(input) {
   445	        wordsToLong(it.groupValues[1])?.let(::comma) ?: it.value
   446	    }
   447	
   448	    private fun dosageRuns(input: String): String {
   449	        val oneToNine = units.filterValues { it in 1..9 }.keys.joinToString("|")
   450	        val dosageUnits = "milligram|milligrams|mg|milliliter|milliliters|ml"
   451	        return Regex("(?i)\\b($numberNoAndAlt)\\s+($oneToNine)(?=\\s+(?:$dosageUnits)\\b)").replace(input) {
   452	            val digit = units[it.groupValues[2].lowercase()] ?: return@replace it.value
   453	            "${it.groupValues[1]} $digit"
   454	        }
   455	    }
   456	
   457	    private fun cardinals(input: String): String = Regex("(?i)\\b(?:$numberNoAndAlt)(?:\\s+(?:$numberAlt))*\\b").replace(input) {
   458	        val words = it.value.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")).toMutableList()
   459	        var trailingAnd = false
   460	        if (words.lastOrNull()?.equals("and", ignoreCase = true) == true) {
   461	            words.removeLast()
   462	            trailingAnd = true
   463	        }
   464	        val cardinalText = words.joinToString(" ")
   465	        val value = wordsToLong(cardinalText) ?: return@replace it.value
   466	        val after = input.substring(it.range.last + 1).trimStart()
   467	        val next = after.takeWhile(Char::isLetter).lowercase()
   468	        val before = input.substring(0, it.range.first).trimEnd()
   469	        val sentenceInitial = before.isBlank() || before.lastOrNull()?.let { it in ".!?\n\"'([" } == true
   470	        val titleLike = words.drop(1).any { word -> word.firstOrNull()?.isUpperCase() == true }
   471	        val firstCapital = words.firstOrNull()?.firstOrNull()?.isUpperCase() == true
   472	        val allCaps = cardinalText.any(Char::isLetter) && cardinalText == cardinalText.uppercase()
   473	        val inputLetters = input.filter(Char::isLetter)
   474	        val shout = inputLetters.length > 1 && inputLetters == inputLetters.uppercase()
   475	        val beforeWord = before.takeLastWhile(Char::isLetter)
   476	        val afterWord = after.takeWhile(Char::isLetter)
   477	        val adjacentAllCaps = listOf(beforeWord, afterWord).any { word ->
   478	            word.length > 1 && word == word.uppercase()
   479	        }
   480	        val capitalizedSingleTitle = firstCapital && words.size == 1 && afterWord.firstOrNull()?.isUpperCase() == true
   481	        val hyphenWord = after.removePrefix("-").takeWhile(Char::isLetter)
   482	        val capitalizedHyphenTitle = after.startsWith("-") && hyphenWord.firstOrNull()?.isUpperCase() == true &&
   483	            (hyphenWord.lowercase() in numberWords || hyphenWord.lowercase() in ordinals)
   484	        val midSentenceCapital = firstCapital && !sentenceInitial && !allCaps
   485	        val afterWords = after.split(Regex("\\s+")).filter(String::isNotEmpty)
   486	        val modifiedUnit = next in setOf("square", "cubic") && afterWords.getOrNull(1)?.lowercase() in unitNouns
   487	        val age = next in agePeriods && afterWords.getOrNull(1)?.equals("old", ignoreCase = true) == true
   488	        val hyphenatedUnit = after.startsWith("-") && after.substringBefore(' ').split('-')
   489	            .any { word -> word.lowercase() in unitNouns }
   490	        val force = next in unitNouns || modifiedUnit || age || hyphenatedUnit ||
   491	            Regex("(?i)^-(?:year|month|week|day)s?-old\\b").containsMatchIn(after)
   492	        val replacement = if (value >= 10 || force) comma(value) else it.value
   493	        when {
   494	            (titleLike || capitalizedSingleTitle || capitalizedHyphenTitle) && !shout ||
   495	                midSentenceCapital || allCaps && adjacentAllCaps && !shout -> it.value
   496	            replacement == it.value -> it.value
   497	            trailingAnd -> "$replacement and"
   498	            else -> replacement
   499	        }
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    private fun keepMagnitude(input: String): String = Regex("(\\$)?(\\d{1,3}(?:,\\d{3})+)(?!\\.\\d)(?!,\\d)").replace(input) {
   503	        val value = it.groupValues[2].replace(",", "").toLongOrNull() ?: return@replace it.value
   504	        for ((word, scale) in listOf("trillion" to 1_000_000_000_000L, "billion" to 1_000_000_000L, "million" to 1_000_000L)) {
   505	            val thousandth = scale / 1_000
   506	            if (value >= scale && value % thousandth == 0L) {
   507	                val coefficient = BigDecimal(value).divide(BigDecimal(scale)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()
   508	                if (coefficient.toBigDecimalOrNull()?.let { n -> n < BigDecimal(1_000) } == true) {
   509	                    return@replace "${it.groupValues[1]}$coefficient $word"
   510	                }
   511	            }
   512	        }
   513	        it.value
   514	    }

     1	package com.envi.wispr.insertion
     2	
     3	internal object InsertionText {
     4	    private const val CONTEXT_LIMIT = 64
     5	
     6	    data class SmartPayloadPlan(
     7	        val text: String,
     8	        val changesDictatedText: Boolean,
     9	    )
    10	
    11	    /**
    12	     * Conservatively repairs only unambiguous editor seams. The full editor text is never
    13	     * retained or logged, and only a small window on either side of the selection is inspected.
    14	     */
    15	    fun smartPayload(
    16	        existing: String,
    17	        inserted: String,
    18	        selectionStart: Int,
    19	        selectionEnd: Int,
    20	    ): String = smartPayloadPlan(existing, inserted, selectionStart, selectionEnd).text
    21	
    22	    fun smartPayloadPlan(
    23	        existing: String,
    24	        inserted: String,
    25	        selectionStart: Int,
    26	        selectionEnd: Int,
    27	    ): SmartPayloadPlan {
    28	        if (inserted.isBlank()) return SmartPayloadPlan(inserted, false)
    29	        if (selectionStart !in 0..existing.length ||
    30	            selectionEnd !in selectionStart..existing.length
    31	        ) {
    32	            return SmartPayloadPlan(inserted, false)
    33	        }
    34	        val start = selectionStart
    35	        val end = selectionEnd
    36	        val left = existing.substring(0, start).takeLast(CONTEXT_LIMIT)
    37	        val right = existing.substring(end).take(CONTEXT_LIMIT)
    38	
    39	        // A caret inside an existing word is ambiguous. Preserve the dictated text exactly.
    40	        if (start == end) {
    41	            val betweenWordCharacters = left.lastOrNull()?.isLetterOrDigit() == true &&
    42	                right.firstOrNull()?.isLetterOrDigit() == true
    43	            val afterWordJoiner = left.lastOrNull()?.let { it in "'\u2019-" } == true &&
    44	                left.dropLast(1).lastOrNull()?.isLetterOrDigit() == true
    45	            val beforeWordJoiner = right.firstOrNull()?.let { it in "'\u2019-" } == true &&
    46	                right.drop(1).firstOrNull()?.isLetterOrDigit() == true
    47	            if (betweenWordCharacters || afterWordJoiner || beforeWordJoiner) {
    48	                return SmartPayloadPlan(inserted, false)
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        var payload = removeDuplicateSeamWord(inserted, left, fromStart = true)
    53	        payload = removeDuplicateSeamWord(payload, right, fromStart = false)
    54	        payload = capitalizeAtSentenceStart(payload, left)
    55	
    56	        val first = payload.firstOrNull()
    57	        val leftLast = left.lastOrNull()
    58	        if (leftLast != null && !leftLast.isWhitespace() && first != null &&
    59	            !first.isWhitespace() && !attachesToLeft(first) && !opensRight(leftLast)
    60	        ) {
    61	            payload = " $payload"
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        val last = payload.lastOrNull()
    65	        val rightFirst = right.firstOrNull()
    66	        val needsTrailingSpace = last != null && !last.isWhitespace() &&
    67	            !opensRight(last) && when {
    68	                rightFirst == null -> true
    69	                rightFirst.isWhitespace() -> false
    70	                attachesToLeft(rightFirst) -> false
    71	                else -> true
    72	            }
    73	        if (needsTrailingSpace) payload += " "
    74	        return SmartPayloadPlan(payload, payload != inserted)
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    fun mergeAtSelection(
    78	        existing: String,
    79	        inserted: String,
    80	        selectionStart: Int,
    81	        selectionEnd: Int,
    82	    ): String {
    83	        val start = selectionStart.coerceIn(0, existing.length)
    84	        val end = selectionEnd.coerceIn(start, existing.length)
    85	        return existing.substring(0, start) + inserted + existing.substring(end)
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    private fun capitalizeAtSentenceStart(value: String, left: String): String {
    89	        val trimmedLeft = left.trimEnd()
    90	        val sentenceStart = left.lastOrNull()?.let { it in "\n\r" } == true ||
    91	            trimmedLeft.isEmpty() || trimmedLeft.last() in ".!?"
    92	        if (!sentenceStart) return value
    93	        val index = value.indexOfFirst(Char::isLetter)
    94	        if (index < 0 || value[index].isUpperCase()) return value
    95	        return value.replaceRange(index, index + 1, value[index].uppercase())
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    private fun removeDuplicateSeamWord(value: String, context: String, fromStart: Boolean): String {
    99	        val word = if (fromStart) {
   100	            Regex("[\\p{L}\\p{N}']+$").find(context)?.value
   101	        } else {
   102	            Regex("^[\\p{L}\\p{N}']+").find(context)?.value
   103	        } ?: return value
   104	        val match = if (fromStart) {
   105	            Regex("^[\\p{L}\\p{N}']+").find(value)
   106	        } else {
   107	            Regex("[\\p{L}\\p{N}']+$").find(value)
   108	        } ?: return value
   109	        if (!word.equals(match.value, ignoreCase = true)) return value
   110	        return value.removeRange(match.range).trimStartIf(fromStart).trimEndIf(!fromStart)
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    private fun String.trimStartIf(enabled: Boolean): String = if (enabled) trimStart() else this
   114	    private fun String.trimEndIf(enabled: Boolean): String = if (enabled) trimEnd() else this
   115	
   116	    private fun attachesToLeft(char: Char): Boolean = char in ".,!?;:%)]}'\"\u2019\u201D-/"
   117	    private fun opensRight(char: Char): Boolean = char in "([{'\"\u2018\u201C-/"
   118	}
  1170	    /**
  1171	     * The session owner's own fallback, published under the deterministic label with the reason
  1172	     * logged by name. Closes the ledger and cancels the open request first, so a late engine outcome
  1173	     * cannot be accepted after it and an abandoned cloud call does not run on.
  1174	     */
  1175	    private fun publishFallback(rawText: String, takePreferences: SessionPreferences, reason: PolishReason) {
  1176	        cancelOpenPolishRequest()
  1177	        log.warn("Polish fell back on the session owner: reason=$reason")
  1178	        publishResult(
  1179	            deterministicFallback(rawText, takePreferences),
  1180	            PolishEngineLabels.DETERMINISTIC,
  1181	            0,
  1182	            reason,
  1183	            0,
  1184	            PolishContext.from(takePreferences.policy),
  1185	        )
  1186	    }
  1187	
  1188	    /**
  1189	     * The same deterministic pipeline the engine runs with polish off, so the text a user gets
  1190	     * cannot depend on which side failed (issue #69; the regex polisher that used to run here
  1191	     * capitalised sentences and appended a period the engine never did).
  1192	     */
  1193	    private fun deterministicFallback(
  1194	        rawText: String,
  1195	        takePreferences: SessionPreferences,
  1196	    ): String {
  1197	        val prepared = restoreTakeVocabulary(rawText, takePreferences)
  1198	        // The engine resolves the same answer on its own side. Detecting here too is what keeps this
  1199	        // terminal from being the one that still applies English rules to foreign words when the engine
  1200	        // is the side that failed (#107); the alternative was a new AIDL transaction to carry it across,
  1201	        // which `workflow-process.md` RULE: tier-routing classifies as REFACTOR for a limb feature.
  1202	        val cleaned = PolishFallback.deterministic(prepared, takePreferences.cleanup, languageDetector)
  1203	        return restoreTakeVocabulary(cleaned, takePreferences)
  1204	    }

  1206	    /**
  1207	     * Closes the ledger and cancels exactly the request that was open, if any. Called as every

  1215	    }

  1436	    /** @return whether the words actually reached the clipboard, which the copy depends on. */
  1437	    private suspend fun keepOnClipboard(
  1438	        transcriptId: Long,
  1439	        text: String,
  1440	    ): Boolean {
  1441	        val copied = host.copyToClipboard(text)
  1442	        if (transcriptId <= 0L) return copied
  1443	
  1444	        historyWrites.enqueue("clipboard-only outcome") { repository ->
  1445	            repository.finalizeInsertionOutcome(
  1446	                transcriptId,
  1447	                TranscriptEntity.STATUS_INSERTION_INTERRUPTED,
  1448	                if (copied) InsertionResults.CLIPBOARD else InsertionResults.INSERTION_FAILED,
  1449	                interrupted = true,
  1450	            )
  1451	        }
  1452	        return copied
  1453	    }

  1455	    /**
  1456	     * Tells the user where their words went, in one calm line and nothing else.
  1457	     *
  1458	     * Whether to speak at all is `FallbackAnnouncement`'s decision, not this method's: a user
  1459	     * who never granted the permission is in clipboard-only mode by choice and gets nothing. What
  1460	     * it says is a measured destination and never an inferred fault, which is why there is no
  1461	     * failure haptic and nothing left in the shade: this is an ordinary outcome of a working
  1462	     * product, not an error.
  1463	     */
  1464	    private fun announceInsertionFallback(
  1465	        handoff: InsertionHandoff,
  1466	        clipboard: ClipboardOutcome,

  1469	        val announcement = FallbackAnnouncement.fallbackAnnouncement(
  1470	            autoPaste = host.autoPasteAvailability(),
  1471	            handoff = handoff,
  1472	            clipboard = clipboard,
  1473	            savedInHistory = savedInHistory,
  1474	        ) ?: return
  1475	        host.postToMain {
  1476	            host.toastFromService(announcement.line)
  1477	        }
  1478	    }

  1480	    private fun keepInHistoryOnly(transcriptId: Long) {

  1486	                InsertionResults.HISTORY_ONLY,
  1487	                interrupted = true,
  1488	            )
  1489	        }
  1490	    }

  1492	    private fun cancelRecording() {

  1494	            if (!state.compareAndSet(SessionState.RECORDING, SessionState.CANCELLING)) return
  1495	            surface.showProcessing()
  1496	            arbiter.reserve(Claimants.CANCEL)

  1498	        cancelCaptureAndFinish(cancel, TerminalReason.CANCELLED_RECORDING)
  1499	    }

     4	internal enum class PipelineOutcome {
     5	    /** Deterministic cleanup returned the original text; the model was not consulted. */
     6	    CLEANUP_RECOVERED,
     7	    /** Cleanup left nothing to polish; the model was not consulted. */
     8	    EMPTY_AFTER_CLEANUP,
     9	    /** No model was offered. */
    10	    NO_MODEL,
    11	    /** Three words or fewer (or under ten characters in a script without spaces): never sent to a model (#2). */

    13	    /** The model returned null, blank, or threw. */
    14	    MODEL_DECLINED,
    15	    /** The model returned text that failed the safety check. */
    16	    MODEL_REJECTED,
    17	    MODEL_ACCEPTED,
    18	}
    19	
    20	internal data class PolishPipelineResult(
    21	    val text: String,
    22	    val usedModel: Boolean,
    23	    val recovered: Boolean,
    24	    val outcome: PipelineOutcome,
    25	    /** Which guard refused the model's output (counts only, never text), for the log; null otherwise. */
    26	    val refusal: String? = null,
    27	)
    28	
    29	/** Pure ordering boundary for cleanup, optional S1, safety validation, and spelling restoration. */
    30	internal object PolishPipeline {
    31	    fun run(
    32	        rawText: String,
    33	        options: CleanupOptions = CleanupOptions(),
    34	        language: CleanupLanguage = CleanupLanguage.Unknown,
    35	        model: ((cleanedText: String) -> String?)? = null,
    36	    ): PolishPipelineResult {
    37	        val cleanup = DeterministicCleanup.apply(rawText, options, language)
    38	        val cleaned = cleanup.text
    39	        val fallback = cleaned
    40	        if (cleanup.recovered) {
    41	            return PolishPipelineResult(fallback, usedModel = false, recovered = true, outcome = PipelineOutcome.CLEANUP_RECOVERED)
    42	        }
    43	        if (cleaned.isBlank()) {
    44	            return PolishPipelineResult(cleaned, usedModel = false, recovered = false, outcome = PipelineOutcome.EMPTY_AFTER_CLEANUP)
    45	        }
    46	        if (model == null) {
    47	            return PolishPipelineResult(fallback, usedModel = false, recovered = false, outcome = PipelineOutcome.NO_MODEL)
    48	        }
    49	        if (tooShortForPolish(cleaned)) {
    50	            return PolishPipelineResult(fallback, usedModel = false, recovered = false, outcome = PipelineOutcome.TOO_SHORT)
    51	        }
    52	        val candidate = runCatching { model.invoke(cleaned)?.substringAfterLast("</think>")?.trim() }.getOrNull()
    53	        if (candidate.isNullOrBlank()) {
    54	            return PolishPipelineResult(fallback, usedModel = false, recovered = true, outcome = PipelineOutcome.MODEL_DECLINED)
    55	        }
    56	        val refusal = TextSafety.refusal(cleaned, candidate)
    57	        if (refusal != null) {
    58	            return PolishPipelineResult(fallback, usedModel = false, recovered = true, outcome = PipelineOutcome.MODEL_REJECTED, refusal = refusal)
    59	        }
    60	        return PolishPipelineResult(candidate, usedModel = true, recovered = false, outcome = PipelineOutcome.MODEL_ACCEPTED)
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    /**
    64	     * The Mac's too-short bypass (`LLMPolishStep.minWordsForPolish`, `minCharsForCJKPolish`): models
    65	     * hallucinate on ultra-short input ("Yeah" into an essay). Three words or fewer pass through; text in a
    66	     * script without spaces (CJK, Thai, Lao) is judged by characters, under ten passes through.
    67	     */
    68	    internal fun tooShortForPolish(cleaned: String): Boolean {
    69	        // Code points, not chars: a supplementary-plane Han character is a surrogate pair that no Char test sees.
    70	        val letters = cleaned.codePoints().toArray().filter { Character.isLetter(it) }
    71	        if (letters.isNotEmpty() && letters.count { isSpacelessScript(it) } * 2 >= letters.size) {
    72	            return letters.size < MIN_CHARS_SPACELESS
    73	        }
    74	        return cleaned.split(Regex("\\s+")).count { it.isNotEmpty() } <= MIN_WORDS_FOR_POLISH
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    private fun isSpacelessScript(codePoint: Int): Boolean = when (Character.UnicodeScript.of(codePoint)) {
    78	        Character.UnicodeScript.HAN, Character.UnicodeScript.HIRAGANA, Character.UnicodeScript.KATAKANA,
    79	        Character.UnicodeScript.HANGUL, Character.UnicodeScript.THAI, Character.UnicodeScript.LAO,
    80	        -> true
    81	        else -> false
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    private const val MIN_WORDS_FOR_POLISH = 3
    85	    private const val MIN_CHARS_SPACELESS = 10
    86	}

    11	/**
    12	 * The pinned editor as the service can see it on one tick. `null` from [EditorWrites.locateTarget]
    13	 * means it is not present right now; every field here is a read the service made for this tick.

    16	    val read: EditorRead,
    17	    val selection: EditorSelection?,

    20	
    21	/** What the editor's paste call reported, classified by the port that made it. */
    22	internal enum class PasteOutcome {
    23	    /** `performAction` returned true. */
    24	    ACCEPTED,
    25	
    26	    /** `performAction` returned false: the framework's explicit rejection, nothing mutated. */
    27	    REFUSED,
    28	
    29	    /** The pinned node was not there to be asked; `performAction` was never called. */
    30	    TARGET_GONE,
    31	
    32	    /**
    33	     * The editor's text or selection no longer matches the snapshot the payload was composed against;
    34	     * `performAction` was never called. The attempt prepares again on its next tick.
    35	     */
    36	    CONTEXT_CHANGED,
    37	}
    38	
    39	/** What the input connection's commit call reported, classified by the port that made it. */
    40	internal enum class CommitOutcome {
    41	    /** `commitText` was called on the captured connection. Void by contract. */
    42	    SENT,

    44	    /**

    47	     */

    49	}

    51	/**

    53	 * with its `AccessibilityNodeInfo`, input connection and clipboard calls; a test implements it with a
    54	 * fake. Any of these may throw; the attempt catches and classifies by WHEN the throw happened, never
    55	 * by what it was.
    56	 */
    57	internal interface EditorWrites {
    58	    fun locateTarget(): TargetState?
    59	
    60	    /**
    61	     * Whether the input session provably belongs to the pinned node right now. `true` also captures
    62	     * that session inside the port, so [readSurrounding] and [commit] use the connection that was
    63	     * checked, never whichever one is current later.
    64	     */
    65	    fun commitEligible(): Boolean
    66	
    67	    /**
    68	     * A read off the captured input connection: up to [beforeChars] characters before the caret and
    69	     * [afterChars] after it. Null when no session was captured, the captured one is no longer live,
    70	     * or the editor answered nothing.
    71	     */
    72	    fun readSurrounding(beforeChars: Int, afterChars: Int): SurroundingWindow?
    73	
    74	    /**

    77	     * CONFIRMED ownership of the clipboard: a read Android refuses is not a licence to write over a
    78	     * clip the user may have copied since, and it is not a licence to paste an unknown clip either,
    79	     * so `false` there ends the attempt with the words kept wherever they are.
    80	     */
    81	    fun stageClipboard(payload: String): Boolean
    82	
    83	    /**

    85	     * node, compare its text and selection with the snapshot the payload was composed against
    86	     * ([expectedBaseline], [expectedSelection]), confirm `ACTION_PASTE` is advertised, then paste.
    87	     * [PasteOutcome.TARGET_GONE] and [PasteOutcome.CONTEXT_CHANGED] mean `performAction` was never

    98	    fun commit(payload: String): CommitOutcome

   115	/**

   134	    enum class Returned { NONE, TRUE, FALSE, VOID, THREW }

   136	    enum class Evidence { NONE, SURROUNDING, NODE, UNREADABLE, COMMIT_TRUSTED }

   138	    sealed interface Tick {

   142	        data class Verified(val route: InsertionRoute) : Tick
   143	
   144	        data object Sensitive : Tick
   145	
   146	        /** The editor refused the paste (`false`). Nothing was mutated; the clipboard holds the words. */
   147	        data object Rejected : Tick
   148	
   149	        /** The clipboard could not be staged; nothing was written. */
   150	        data object StagingFailed : Tick

   152	        /** The deadline passed. [written] says whether a write was sent and is still unconfirmed. */
   153	        data class Expired(val written: Boolean) : Tick
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    var attempts: Int = 0
   157	        private set
   158	    var route: InsertionRoute? = null
   159	        private set
   160	    var verification: Verification? = null
   161	        private set
   162	    var returned: Returned = Returned.NONE
   163	        private set
   164	    var evidence: Evidence = Evidence.NONE
   165	        private set
   166	    var lastJudgement: Judgement? = null
   167	        private set
   168	    var overrun: Boolean = false
   169	        private set
   170	    var writeCount: Int = 0
   171	        private set

   173	    // A read at any point returned a complete but wrong field (the editor changed or dropped the
   174	    // payload). It latches, because a single MISS is real evidence of failure that a later unreadable
   175	    // tick must not erase: a commit is trusted on expiry ONLY when every judgement was UNREADABLE.

   193	    fun tick(): Tick {

   195	        if (expired()) return expiredTick()

   200	    private fun prepareAndWrite(): Tick {
